# UI consistency audit 2026-09-07 — finding inventory

Every merged Phase-1 finding (613 after de-duplication of 638 raw), with the reader's evidence and proposed fix and, where the finding went through the refute-by-default verification pass, the verdict. This is the tracked copy of the inventory the report (`ui-consistency-audit-2026-09-07.md`) is built from; the 364 findings that were not verified are marked `not verified` and should be treated as reader-reported until a fix PR re-reads their lines. F0009 is folded into F0008 (same line, same defect).

| # | Sev (final) | Cat | Reader:id | Location | Finding | Evidence | Proposed fix | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
